Model before rewriting:

find a: matrix indexed by [int(1..3), int(2..4)] of int(1..5)
find i: int(2..4)

such that

a[1, 2] = 1,
a[1, 3] = 1,
a[1, 4] = 1,
a[2, 3] = 1,
a[2, 4] = 1,
a[3, 2] = 1,
a[3, 4] = 1,
a[i, i] = i

i
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
i
new variables:
  find i#order_1: bool
  find i#order_2: bool
  find i#order_3: bool
new constraints:
  i#order_1
  or([!(i#order_2),i#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(i#order_3),i#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [i#order_1,i#order_2,i#order_3;int(1..)] [2, 4]) >= 2,SATInt(Order, [i#order_1,i#order_2,i#order_3;int(1..)] [2, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
